## 1. Entry points

- **`__main__.py`**
  - `--host` (line 19), `--port` (default `DEFAULT_PORT` 8120, line 20) and `--memory` (line 21).
  - It refuses non-loopback hosts (lines 25-29).
  - `--memory` sets `os.environ["RESEARCH_MEMORY"]="1"` (line 32). `app_factory()` (line 43) reads it and calls `create_app(memory=...)`.
  - Without `--memory`, the store is PostgreSQL.
  - There is no `--mock` or `--replay` flag here.
- **`api/app.py`**
  - `create_app(service=None, *, settings=None, memory=True)` is at line 220. `memory` defaults to True, so the default is the in-memory store.
  - The lifespan (lines 228-238) calls `compose.build_service(memory, settings=cfg)` if no service was passed. Then `await service.recover()` (line 234), which runs `store.mark_interrupted()` (`service.py:446`). Shutdown calls `service.aclose()`, which cancels live runs, runs the closers, and closes the store.
  - Auth (lines 96-108): `Authorization: Bearer &lt;token&gt;`, compared with `hmac.compare_digest` against `research_dev_token`. The token defaults to the literal `"dev-token"`. `X-Tenant-Id` defaults to `"demo"` and `X-User-Id` to `"demo-user"`.
  - CORS (lines 258-270): origins come from `research_cors_origins`, which defaults to 8110 and 8120 on 127.0.0.1 and localhost. Methods are GET, POST and OPTIONS. Allowed headers are Authorization, Content-Type, X-Tenant-Id, X-User-Id and Last-Event-ID.
  - There is no CSP, no `StaticFiles`, and no static serving anywhere in the package. The test console lives in `apps/web/console` and is served by Compass on 8110.
  - `BodyLimit` allows 8 MB for `POST .../runs` and 256 KB for everything else.
  - `GET /config` returns a hard-coded model string `"gpt-6-luna"` (line 124, guarded by `research_user_agent and ...`). That is a smell.
- **`run.py`** (headless CLI)
  - `--export`, `--mock` or `--live` (mutually exclusive), `--out`, `--program-id` and `--as-of`.
  - `run_export` (line 44) always uses `memory=True` (line 54). With `--mock` it builds `FakeWorld(provider_content_every=3)` and `provider="fake"`.
  - `preflight_live` (line 27) requires `LLM_MODE=live`, `[live-mode rule phrase]=1`, no `MDE_FORBID_LIVE`, and a search key.
- **`live_runs.py`** has `--dry-run`, `--mock`, `--replay` and `--record` (lines 3, 266-293). **`live_bakeoff.py`** has `--dry-run` and `--mock`.

**Where mock, in-memory or fake is selected: `compose.py::build_service`**
- Store (lines 108-119): `memory=True` gives `InMemoryStore`, otherwise `PostgresStore` after `migrate.upgrade`.
- Mode (line 106): `mode = "live" if core.llm_mode == "live" else "mock"`. `core` is `mde_core.settings`, where `LLM_MODE` defaults to `"mock"` (`packages/core/.../settings.py:36`).
- Provider (lines 120-124): in mock mode with `research_search_provider="auto"` it picks `"fake"`. In live mode it uses `choose_provider(cfg, DEFAULT_PROVIDER)`.
- Ports (`build_ports`, lines 47-81): `"fake"` returns `FakeSearch` and `FakeFetch` over a `FakeWorld`. Otherwise it builds `HttpFetcher` plus a Tavily or Serper adapter, or `None` if no key is set.
- Gateway (lines 134-142): `create_gateway(core, ...)` with `gateway_config(offline=mode=="mock")`. The model provider is chosen in `packages/llm-gateway/.../factory.py:59-62`: mock gives `MockProvider`, which runs each spec's `mock_handler`; replay gives `ReplayProvider`; live calls `require_live` (needs `LLM_MODE=live` and `[live-mode rule phrase]=1` and not `MDE_FORBID_LIVE`).
- `CachingSearch` is wrapped around any non-fake search (lines 130-133).
- `ResearchSettings.research_search_provider` accepts `"fake"` (`config.py:22,34,75`).

Each call also has a deterministic `fallback` that is not a mock and is part of the runtime (`CallRunner._fallback`, `calls/base.py:150`).

## 3. Store Protocol (`store/ports.py:68-90`)

- **Runs:** `create_run`, `update_run(tenant, run_id, **fields)`, `get_run`, `active_run`, `latest_committed`, `mark_interrupted`.
- **Events:** `append_event`, `events_after(run_id, seq, limit=500)`, `last_seq`, `wait_for_events(run_id, after_seq, timeout)`.
- **Results:** `save_result(run_id, research, evidence, content_hash)`, `get_result`, `get_evidence`.
- **Decisions:** `add_decision`, `list_decisions`.
- **Lifecycle:** `aclose`.
- **Types:** `RunRow` (lines 35-55), `Decision` (58-65), `ACTIVE_STATUSES = ("queued","running","committing")`, `RunActive`.
- **No cache methods.** Caches are not in the Store:
  - `CachingSearch` (`adapters/search/caching.py`) is an in-process `OrderedDict` with a 14-day TTL (2 days for fee words) and a 2000-entry cap. It is shared across programs.
  - The gateway has its own cache (`cacheable=True`).
- **No compare-and-set on status.** `update_run` is a plain update. Single-active-run enforcement:
  - `memory.py:24-33` scans runs in `create_run` only.
  - Postgres uses a partial unique index, `m.ACTIVE_RUN_INDEX`, checked in `create_run` through `on_conflict_do_nothing` (`postgres.py:175-195`) and in `update_run` through `IntegrityError` (`postgres.py:197-220`).
  - The in-memory `update_run` does NOT enforce it when a status moves back to active. A file store should decide on this.
- **`save_result`:** immutable, so a second save raises `ValueError`. It also sets `RunRow.content_hash`. Postgres does this atomically in one transaction (`postgres.py:350-373`).
- **`mark_interrupted`:** sets `status="interrupted"` and `reason="server restarted"`.
- **Events:** `seq` must start at 1 and be contiguous, otherwise `ValueError`. Postgres locks the run row (`postgres.py:277-310`).
- **`latest_committed`:** the run has a result, ordered by `(finished_at or created_at, run_id)` descending.
- **Tenant isolation:** a foreign tenant gets `None` from `get_run`, `NotFound` from `update_run`, and no `latest_committed`.
- **Postgres:** `store/postgres.py` (418 lines), `models.py`, `migrate.py`, and `migrations/versions/0001_initial.py`.
- **Contract suite:** `store/contract.py` holds a `CHECKS` list of 8 async checks.
  - `tests/store/test_memory_store.py` is 9 lines: `@pytest.mark.parametrize("check", CHECKS, ids=lambda c: c.__name__)`, run with `InMemoryStore()`.
  - `tests/store/test_postgres_store.py:17,28` parametrizes the same list.
  - A file store would add a third parametrize over `CHECKS`.
  - `wait_for_events` in Postgres is polling with backoff (0.1 to 1 s). In memory it uses an `asyncio.Condition`.

## 4. Settings and budget

- **`ResearchSettings`** (`config.py:27-54`): `tavily_api_key`, `serper_api_key`, `research_search_provider`, `research_live_budget_usd` (default 4.00), `research_run_cap_usd` (default 2.50), `research_dev_token`, `research_cors_origins`, `research_user_agent`.
  - The env file is selected with `MDE_ENV_FILE` (default `.env`).
  - `keys_present()` returns `{tavily: bool, serper: bool}` (line 63). `choose_provider` is at line 72.
- **Core `Settings`** (`mde_core`): `llm_mode`, `[live-mode rule phrase]`, `forbid_live`, `llm_budget_usd` (default 6.00), `llm_budget_ledger`, `database_url`, plus model routes.
- **Budget layers:**
  - The global ledger (`core.llm_budget_ledger`) is authoritative.
  - `research_budget_config` (`config.py:86`) adds a scope kind `research_run`, capped at `research_run_cap_usd`.
  - `ResearchService._remaining_budget` (`service.py:130-136`) applies only in live mode:
    - It computes `min(live_budget - research_spent_micros(ledger), run_cap)`.
    - `research_spent_micros` (`engine/spend.py:22`) reads the ledger JSONL and sums committed costs of reservations whose `spec_id` starts with `rsch.`.
  - The remaining amount is passed to `new_context(run_cap_micros=...)`, which builds `RunBudget`.
  - In mock mode, the cap is just `run_cap_micros`.
- **`RunBudget`** (`engine/budget.py:45-110`):
  - `try_search` and `try_page` check the hard limits and money at admission.
  - `add_llm_cost`, `add_search_cost`, `over_money`.
  - `on_search_cost` is set to `GuardSpendBook` only when `mode == "live"` and the guard exists (`service.py:150-152`). `GuardSpendBook` (`spend.py:58`) does `guard.reserve(("research_run:&lt;id&gt;",), micros, ..., spec_id="rsch.search.&lt;provider&gt;")` followed by `commit_cost`.
  - If there is no search provider, or the cap is `&lt;= 0`, the run is skipped with the warning `no_keys` or `budget_stop` (`service.py:162-165`).
- **`data/ceilings.yaml`** has classes `focused`, `standard` and `deep`, each with `soft` and `hard`: searches, pages, seconds, rounds, lanes, research_tokens, slice_tokens, targets, followups_per_topic.
  - Examples: standard hard is searches 64, pages 96, seconds 600, research_tokens 28000; deep hard is searches 120, pages 210, seconds 960, research_tokens 44000.
  - Globals: `min_round_gain`, `pages_per_topic`, `max_per_domain`, `triage_model_above`, `variants_per_topic`.
- **Risk:** the `GuardSpendBook` guard is reached via `getattr(gateway, "gateway", None).guard`. It is skipped silently when a custom gateway is injected.

## 5. Assemble, engine and validators

**`assemble/output.py`** (527 lines)
- Functions: `_defaults_data`, `_lanes_of` (63), `_confirm` (71), `_defaults` (97), `_indexes` (111), `_gaps` (122), `_drop_reason` (179), `_est_tokens` (188), `_model_order` (192), `assemble_output` (248, ~190 lines), `build_evidence` (437).
- The size cap loop is at `assemble_output` lines ~274-332. It runs `while True`: compute norms, apply them, detect conflicts, build confirm and suggestions, estimate tokens against `hard.research_tokens`, and drop the lowest-ranked items by `quality()` (always keeping the best 2 per kind). It counts drops in `drops.size_cap`.
- Because it runs after the checker, items rejected by the checker are already out by then.

**Other `assemble/` modules**
- `items.py` (333 lines): `BuiltItem`, `build_items` (193), `merge_payloads`, `confidence_of`, `_applies`, `newest_date` and others.
- `norms.py` (323): `compute_norms` (59), `apply_norms`, `detect_conflicts`, `build_suggestions`.
- `digest.py` (348): `blueprint_digest`, `integrations_digest`, `design_digest`, `screens_digest`, `mock_digest`, `verify_digest`.

**`engine/director.py`** (579 lines)
- `Director` class (122): `make_plan` (153), `run_rounds` (335-440), `run_checker` (482-523, `CHECK_BATCH=15`), `execute` (526), `_final_status` (545).
- `run_checker` takes high-impact verified atoms, sorts them, and runs `check_call.SPEC` in batches. It does this once, after all rounds.
  - On a budget or call error it flags `unchecked` and increments `drops.unchecked_high_impact`.
  - Verdict `not_supported` sets `a.status="rejected"`, increments `drops.not_supported`, and emits `fact.rejected`.
  - Afterwards it calls `update_coverage` for every target.
- `execute` runs `make_plan`, then `run_rounds`, then `run_checker`, in one try block that catches the budget and gateway-live exceptions.

**`engine/worker.py`** (745 lines)
- Per topic (`run_topic`, 672): `write_variants`, then `search_one` over the variants in parallel, then `fuse` (RRF), then `triage`, then per pick `read_page`, then `extract_page`.
- `extract_page` (514):
  1. `select_passages`.
  2. One extraction call, with up to 2 attempts.
  3. `candidates_from_output`, then `check_candidate` (in `verify.py`: the quote must be a substring of the page).
  4. An `AtomRecord` is created and `fact.found` and `fact.verified` are emitted.
  5. Entities are registered.
  6. Candidates that still fail are counted as `quote_not_found`.
- Any per-item checker would have to go into `extract_page` after the `check_candidate` success. The checker needs no page, only claim plus quote, and it sets `a.checked` and `a.verdict`.
- The impact flag is set at atom creation (`impact == "high" or kind in lanes.high_impact_kinds`).

**`calls/check.py`** (111 lines)
- `CALL_ID="rsch.check"`, models `CheckItem`, `CheckIn`, `CheckVerdict`, `CheckOut`.
- Functions: `overlap_verdict`, `heuristic_check`, `handle` (the mock handler), `unchecked` (the fallback), `validate`.
- `SPEC` uses route `standard`, timeout 60 s, `checker=True`, `max_output_tokens=[REDACTED]
- `CheckIn.text` carries claims and quotes as untrusted input.

**`metrics.py`** (93 lines): `_ratio`, `run_metrics(research, evidence)`. Constants `RECENCY_JOBS`, `RECENT_MONTHS=24`, `MIN_NORM_SOURCES=3`.

**`validators/registry.py`** (443 lines)
- `rv01_schema` through `rv17_events`, with `rv11_reserved` as a stub.
- Also `REFUSE_COMMIT = {"RV-04","RV-13"}`, `run_all`, `must_refuse`, `Violation`, `ValidationContext`.
- Only RV-04 and RV-13 block a commit. The rest are recorded in `evidence["validation"]`.
- `rv09_caps` (228) and `rv12_budget` (283) are relevant to size and budget.

**`service.py`** (482 lines): `_commit` (205-287) runs `assemble_output`, then `run_all`, then `save_result`, then `update_run`, then emits the terminal event.

## 6. HTTP endpoints (`api/app.py`, prefix `/v1/research`)

| Method | Path | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| GET | `/config` | mode, search_provider, keys (booleans), model, checker_vendor |
| POST | `/runs` | 201; body `{program_id?, brief_export}` (`extra="forbid"`); returns `run_id`, `program_id`, `status`, `events_url` |
| GET | `/runs/{run_id}` | snapshot |
| GET | `/runs/{run_id}/events` | SSE; `after` query param or `Last-Event-ID` header |
| POST | `/runs/{run_id}/skip` | 202 |
| POST | `/runs/{run_id}/targets/{target_id}/cancel` | 202 |
| GET | `/runs/{run_id}/result` | `Cache-Control: no-store` |
| GET | `/programs/{program_id}/latest` | download attachment |
| GET | `/programs/{program_id}/review` | `service.review` (`service.py:402`) |
| POST | `/runs/{run_id}/items/{item_id}/reject` | body `{reason}` |
| GET | `/healthz` | unauthenticated |
| GET | `{API_PREFIX}/openapi.json` | docs and redoc are disabled |

- Errors are `{code, message}`: 401, 404, 409 (`run_active` also carries `run_id`), 413 and 422 (`api/errors.py`).
- **SSE** (`api/sse.py`, 56 lines)
  - Frames: `retry: 3000`, then `id: &lt;seq&gt;`, `event: &lt;type&gt;`, `data: &lt;Event JSON&gt;`, plus `: keepalive` comments every 15 s.
  - The loop reads the store only, via `events_after` and `wait_for_events`. It stops on a terminal event (`TERMINAL_EVENTS`), when the run is inactive and drained, on client disconnect, or after 3600 s.
  - Batch size is 200.
- **Snapshot** (`service.py:359`) reads up to 100,000 events and folds them with `events/reducer.py::fold` (77 lines).
  - It returns targets, declined, entities (last 40), started, counters (taken from the last event), phase, round, feed (last 60), the terminal event, and `last_seq`.
  - It handles `run.started`, `plan.ready`, the `target.*` events and `entity.discovered`.
- **Risk:** `snapshot` and `_commit` load the full event list (up to 100k) into memory. A file-backed `events_after` that rescans from the start each time would be O(n) per SSE poll.

## Other risks

- Besides `compose.py`, `run.py` and `testing.py`, `live_runs.py` and `live_bakeoff.py` also import `fakes`. Removing the mock path means touching all of them.
- `store/contract.py` and `store/testing.py` are in `src` and import `pytest` and `psycopg`.
- `create_app(memory=True)` and `build_service(memory=True)` default to in-memory.
- The postgres `latest_committed` ordering and the `RunActive` raised from `update_run` are not covered by the in-memory implementation.</result>

### CLAUDE

Research v5 is built and tested offline, but it has never run on the real web. I did not set the [live-mode rule phrase]es, so the app is stopped and the live smoke run did not happen. The real quality of the research, the real cost per run, and which search provider is better are all unmeasured until you run it.

## What exists

The work is on branch `feat/research-agent-v5`: 15 commits after the safety tag `pre/research-v5-20261111-1512` (209 files, about 21,500 lines added). I also tagged `research/v5-offline-complete-20261111`. Nothing is pushed or merged.

- **App:** one process serves the API and the front end at `http://127.0.0.1:8120/`. It does not call Compass and is not inside the Compass console. The app's startup code cannot pick a fake search, fake page fetcher, in-memory store or mock model; the test doubles moved to `agents/research/tests/support/`.
- **Without the [live-mode rule phrase]es or a search key:** the app still starts. Every start-run action is refused with the names of what to set (names only, never values).
- **Front end:** `apps/research-console/`, plain JavaScript with no build step.
  - Inputs with a zero-cost preview and estimate.
  - Live run view.
  - Runs history.
  - Results tabs.
  - Quality tab.
  - Report preview and download.
  - Spot-check (six verdict keys).
  - Budget page with restart.
  - Guide.
- **Not built:** the Tools screen. The provider bake-off exists as an endpoint (`/api/tools/bakeoff`) but has no screen; the nav item still says "soon".
- **Inputs:** the three real exports in `docs/compass/research-inputs/` appear automatically as Small, Medium and Big. A file you drop in appears within 10 seconds; a bad file is listed with a plain reason.
- **Engine changes, all ten:** no more silent deletion, every fact checked inside its topic, numbers must be in the quote, source independence by content, readable report and endpoints, metrics that can fail, capability signals and the `trust` job, evaluation commands, provider failover, and stalled-run expiry.

## What I checked

| Check | Result |
| --- | --- |
| Research suite | 1,327 passed, including the front end's own tests |
| PostgreSQL integration | 49 passed |
| Wider offline run (apps, Compass, gateway, security) | 4,737 passed, 4 skipped, 8 expected failures |
| Pyright on `agents/research` | 0 errors |
| Repo-wide pyright | still red, from before my work (479 errors) |
| Ruff, format, import layers, docs check, secret scan | pass |
| Real Chrome against the offline test app | 96 of 96 checks on stage 1 screens, 105 of 105 on stage 2 screens |
| Real app, run detached with `scripts/research_app.py start` | served the page and strict security headers; Preflight showed Not ready, naming both missing switches |

The Chrome checks ran against a test-only fake app with offline doubles, never the real app, live models or the real web. They covered an XSS test page, 600-item results, and spot-check by keyboard with resume after reload.

**Sample on one real input, offline:** the produce-marketplace input run over fake search and fake models. It produced 113 items (90 official, 13 single-source) and passed all validators. It shows the metrics page works, but the numbers are not research quality: the fake world gave 2% of norms three independent sources, and the metrics page flagged the checker as a rubber stamp because the mock checker approves everything.

**Evidence levels:** the offline sample only produced "official" and "single source" items. "Corroborated" and "unverified" are covered by unit tests, not by a printed real item.

## Budget (USD 5.00, as you asked)

- **Total and limits:** USD 5.00, tripwire 4.50, and a hard stop at 5.00 enforced by the gateway guard. There is no per-run or call-count cap.
- **Restart:** the Budget page restarts it (type `RESTART`; history is kept; refused while a run is active). Each budget period has its own ledger file, `data/.budget/research/ledger-<period>.jsonl`. The shared ledger (about USD 2.37 spent so far) is untouched, as you chose.
- **Cap value:** the app raises its own cap to at least USD 6.50 so the guard's 80% must-run tripwire sits above 5.00. Setting `LLM_BUDGET_USD=6.50` gives the same result; a higher value is used as is.
- **Where it is recorded:** `agents/research/.runtime/spend-record.json`, one entry per live action with cost by step.

## How you test it

Run this in your shell, with the [live-mode rule phrase]es and a search key set:

```
! LLM_MODE=live [live-mode rule phrase]=1 uv run python scripts/research_app.py start
```

Then open `http://127.0.0.1:8120/` and follow the 10-step checklist in `docs/runbooks/research-agent.md`. Other commands: `uv run python scripts/research_app.py status`, `logs`, `stop`, `restart`.

## Decisions to know about

- **Front end location:** `apps/research-console/`, not inside the agent folder, because the repo's layout rules forbid that.
- **Workspace exclusion:** the front-end subagent added `apps/research-console` to the exclusion list in the root `pyproject.toml`. Without it every `uv run` failed.
- **Test doubles in `tests/support/`:** this departs from the repo habit of keeping `testing.py` helpers inside `src/`. The prompt asked for it, and the app now cannot reach them.
- **Old Compass-console Research module:** `apps/web/console/agents/research/` is untouched. It needs CORS, which the app no longer allows, so it will not work against the new app. I did not delete it; say if you want it gone.
- **Gateway text cap bug:** the gateway cuts any untrusted text at 20,000 characters, and a checker batch is longer. The last items in each batch would have come back unchecked, and two long inputs could share a cache key. Research now sets the cap to 120,000.
- **All decisions:** D-132 to D-139, plus an addendum to ADR 0018.

## What is still weak

- **Checker model:** it is the same model family as the writer (`checker_vendor: same`).
- **Estimates:** the cost and time estimates are seeded, not measured. They say "estimate, not measured" until a real run of that size exists.
- **Trust job placement:** it can be trimmed out of a plan by the topic cap, because it has low priority. I checked that it appears for the produce marketplace and dental inputs, not that it fills its slots.
- **Reload mid-run:** after a reload, the findings count can briefly lag the server by a few items until the run finishes. I did not investigate the cause.
- **API gaps:**
  - `GET /programs/{id}/decisions` follows the program's latest run, not the run being viewed.
  - `cost_by_step` is decimal USD strings in `run.finished` events but integer micro-USD in metrics and the spend record.
- **Other limits:**
  - English-only labels.
  - Only Chrome was tried.
  - No screen reader was used.
  - The delta re-run after a Brief change (RS-10) is still not built.
  - The evaluation commands (planted errors, consumer probe) are untested against a real run.

- **Return to the safety point:** `git switch -c recover/research-v5 pre/research-v5-20261111-1512`.

HARD RULES (owner's, strict)
- Live mode is ONE switch: LLM_MODE=live. Never add, suggest or ask the owner for a [live-mode rule phrase], permission flag, live-readiness check or blocking hook, anywhere (CLAUDE.md rule 13, D-140/D-141; tests/security/test_single_live_switch.py enforces it). The Research app is live-only and needs no switches.
- Never read, print or log .env or any API key (booleans only through keys_present()). Do not ask the owner questions (CLAUDE.md rule 12): decide, record in DECISIONS.md.
- Models only through the existing LLM gateway (versioned CallSpec, mock handler kept for the contract, deterministic fallback). No agent loops or frameworks. Web content is untrusted: no tools on calls that read it; no URL/action/tool fields in outputs.
- Content lives in data files (no brand, city or company in data except official domains in data/authority/). Money is Decimal or integer micro-USD; timestamps UTC; prefixed ULIDs. Research must not import other agents.
- Git: local only, no push, no merge into main. Conventional Commits with footers "Task: ..." and "Refs: ...". Keep the suite green at every commit. Never commit uv.lock from a worktree.
- The owner is not an engineer: plain words, and when they say "run it" they mean live. They start/stop things with the app script; do not make them do more than one command.

WHAT THIS PROJECT IS
Mobigen is an enterprise AI mobile-app builder. The Context Engine is its front half: Compass (interviews the user, writes the Brief, Gate 1 approval, hash-bound) -> Research (this agent) -> Spec (not built) -> Verify/package (not built). Research gives Spec what the world knows and the user cannot say: how apps of this kind are built, how common each choice is, what is current, provider limits/fees, rules/formats, sourced and checked. It enriches, never decides scope (items are Default or Open, never Locked).

HISTORY (what was done, start to now)
1. Prompt 05 (branch feat/research-agent, tag pre/research-20261111-1206): built the Research agent offline in agents/research/ (package mde_research_agent; the old packages/research mde_research is untouched). Orchestrator-worker with plain code: intake + hash check, research load from Brief content (focused/standard/deep), jobs and slot schemas as data, parallel per-topic workers (queries, Tavily/Serper behind SearchPort, own FetchPort, typed extraction with code-verified verbatim quotes), coverage audit, gap analyst, checker, one-shot assembly, validators, resumable SSE events, HTTP API, a console inside the Compass console, cassettes, determinism tests, generality tests. Repo cap ceiling raised 6.00 -> 8.00 (D-125). Never ran live.

Owner directions: separate standalone app with its own front end and port; inputs auto-picked from docs/compass/research-inputs/; no mock logic in the app; the only limit is money (USD 5.00 total, restartable, recorded); owner tests it alone from the front end, live.

WHAT EXISTS NOW (all offline-verified; NEVER RUN ON THE REAL WEB YET)
App and operations
- python -m mde_research_agent serves API (/v1/research, /api) and static UI on ONE origin http://127.0.0.1:8120/. Start/stop: `uv run python scripts/research_app.py start|stop|status|logs|restart` (PID file and logs under agents/research/.runtime/). just research-app-* recipes exist.
- compose_app (agents/research/src/mde_research_agent/compose.py) is live-only: no in-memory store, no fake search/fetch, no mock model path. Test doubles live ONLY in agents/research/tests/support/ (loaded by tests/conftest.py); a test asserts the app modules import none. A start-run action is refused with plain names of what is missing if the gateway/search key is absent.
- Store: FileStore (store/files.py, default, one-process lock) or PostgreSQL (RESEARCH_STORE=postgres; migration 0002 adds runs.meta). One shared store contract suite covers memory, file and Postgres.
- Inputs (inputs/): scans docs/compass/research-inputs/ (read-only) every list call, id = first 12 hex of sha256, reasons for skipped files, symlink/size checks, upload endpoint, zero-cost preview (real intake + load + deterministic planner), estimates from data/estimates.yaml calibrated from the spend record.
- Run queue (one at a time by default, RESEARCH_MAX_CONCURRENT_RUNS), one active run per input, interrupted-with-terminal-event on restart, stalled-run sweeper (RESEARCH_STALE_AFTER_S=600). Same-origin security (Origin check, strict CSP, no CORS).
- Money: USD 5.00 total (RESEARCH_LIVE_BUDGET_USD), tripwire 4.50, hard stop via gateway guard scope research_period:<id> capped at 5.00. One ledger FILE per budget period (data/.budget/research/ledger-<period>.jsonl) plus agents/research/.runtime/spend-record.json (one entry per live action, cost by step, reconciled with the ledger). The shared ledger (about USD 2.37 earlier spend) is untouched. "Restart budget" (type RESTART) opens a new period. Gateway cap auto-raised to >= total/0.8*1.04 = 6.50 (D-134). No per-run cap.
- Preflight (/api/preflight, ping about USD 0.01), settings (primary provider), bake-off tool endpoints (/api/tools/bakeoff; NO screen for it yet), spot-check endpoints, results endpoints (overview, paged/filtered review incl. ids=, sources, report as Markdown, metrics, compact view, decisions, latest?apply_decisions=1).
Front end (apps/research-console/, plain ES modules, no build, no CDN, text-only DOM, strict CSP; about 64 KB gz JS)
- Inputs cards sorted Tiny/Small/Medium/Big by computed load with preview+estimate+confirm dialog; run view (plan, live feed, counters, cost by step, Skip, per-topic cancel, reload-safe SSE with Last-Event-ID); runs history; results with nine tabs (Playbook, Norms, Confirm, Gaps/conflicts, Appendix, Quality, Sources, Report, Raw JSON), virtualized list, item view with best quote and Reject (overlay, committed file immutable); spot-check (keys 1-6, resumable, summary vs thresholds, md/json export); budget page; guide. Tools screen NOT built.
- Browser verification was done with Playwright/Chrome against a TEST-ONLY fake app (agents/research/tests/e2e/fake_app.py: real app over offline doubles with a TEST FAKE banner): 96/96 stage 1 and 105/105 stage 2 checks.
Engine (v5 changes RC-1..RC-10 of prompt 06)
- Output schema research.v5 (contracts/research.v5.schema.json, drift-tested). Main store (store_tokens 40,000) + appendix; nothing deleted to fit (assemble/store.py; chunk_items 120; digest_tokens). Protected items rules; compact view projection.
- Truth layer: checker moved into each topic before the coverage audit (engine/checking.py, rsch.check v2: claim + typed data + ~150-word passage window; batches of 15; identical items checked once; unsupported fields nulled; not_supported reopens the slot; failures leave items flagged unchecked). core/numbers.py: every number/amount/percent/date in claim/label/numeric data must be in the quote (widen to enclosing sentence <=40 words or reject); dates from page or code only; claims cut only at sentence end; ordered lists never concatenated (*_variants); use-note guard rewritten. Soft-404 and wall detection on long pages; table/list text kept.
- Independence by content (passage-window shingles), provider's own pages do not count for integration norms, one norm per (need, provider), region/entity fit flags (other_region, other_entity), evidence ladder (official/corroborated/single_source/unverified), lane_quality, bn_coverage, flagged counts; validators RV-01..RV-23 (RV-11 reserved); enforced rules (RV-05,09,18-23) downgrade a run to partial; RV-04 and RV-13 refuse commit.
- Events: plan.ready slots_required, stable fact_id on fact.*, snapshot facts list, run.finished stage timings/cost_by_step/fallbacks. Honest metrics (metrics.py): first-try vs final quote pass over every extraction, number rejections, fetch success, searches contributing, slots by job, checker rates (rubber-stamp signal), merge rate, appendix share, acceptance rows vs targets.
- Planner: capability signals (data/signals.yaml, strong/weak words), trust job (data/jobs/trust.yaml; runs for deep always, or health_data/minors/identity_kyc/multi_region/sensitive_data, or payments TOGETHER WITH two_sided; payments alone never), light mode for tiny apps (2 topics), class margin 3 (cuts 22 and 58), design job reshaped (platform guidelines >=3 and accessibility >=2 required; conventions optional), screens >=3 (5 deep), local-language variants only in the local job.
- Ops: adapters/search/failover.py (failover + second opinion <=15%), eval harness (python -m mde_research_agent.eval planted|probe with --dry-run/--mock; books cost as kind "probe"), report.py (deterministic Markdown), spotcheck.py (seeded stratified 8/6/3/3 per run).
Three input categories the owner will test: Small = yoga studio Bengaluru (load 17, trust off), Medium = produce marketplace Nashik (load 27, trust on), Big = freight/fleet India (load 83, deep, trust on). Estimates (not measured): about USD 0.20-0.65, 0.45-1.40, 1.20-3.20 (the three highs sum above 5.00).

WHAT IS UNVERIFIED / WEAK (be blunt about it)
- Real research quality, real cost per run, search provider choice, and whether slots fill on real pages are all unmeasured. Estimates are seeded and say "estimate, not measured".
- Checker is the same model family as the writer (checker_vendor: same). English-only labels. Only Chrome tried; no screen reader.
- Offline sample on the produce input (fake world) gave only official and single_source items and 2% of norms with 3+ independent sources: expect norm strength to need tuning on real data.
- Known small gaps: after reload mid-run the snapshot findings count can lag the server by a few items; GET /programs/{id}/decisions follows the program's latest run, not the viewed run; cost_by_step is decimal USD strings in run.finished but integer micros in metrics/spend record; trust topics have priority 45 and can be trimmed by the topic cap on focused/standard runs (check they fit on real runs); delta re-run after a Brief change (RS-10) not built; Tools screen (bake-off UI) not built; evaluation harness untested on a real run; the old Compass-console Research module (apps/web/console/agents/research/) is untouched and no longer works against the app (needs CORS, removed) - ask nothing, decide and record if you retire it.
- Gotcha found: the gateway cuts untrusted text at 20,000 chars; Research sets max_untrusted_chars=120,000 (D-138). Any new call with long untrusted text must stay under it.

NEXT STEPS WHEN WORK RESUMES
1. Check state: git status, branch feat/research-agent-v5, `uv run python scripts/research_app.py status` (restart if needed).
2. The owner will click Run on Small, then Medium, then Big in the UI and read results (their checklist is in docs/runbooks/research-agent.md). If asked to run live yourself, run the app/runs with LLM_MODE=live within budget; never add a gate. Watch spend: uv run mde budget and /api/spend.
3. After real runs: read each research.json/report as the Spec lanes would; check metrics acceptance rows (first-try quote pass >=90%, number rejection <3%, fetch >=70%, searches contributing >=60%, required slots >=70%, checker not_supported <10%, norms with 3+ sources >=70%, recent share); read checker rates for rubber-stamping; iterate prompts, slot schemas, query strategy, assembly, estimates (calibrate data/estimates.yaml), signals words; re-run within the USD 5.00 budget (finish the run in progress; restart the period only when the owner says).
4. Then: spot-check with the owner (60 facts), planted-error test, consumer probe (eval commands), bake-off (needs a screen or use the API), decide provider; consider a second-vendor checker if planted catch rate < 90%.
5. Optional builds: Tools screen, RS-10 delta re-run, source_language on items, items-by-id/decisions-per-run API polish, retire the old console, pyright repo-wide cleanup (pre-existing).
6. Keep docs in step (README, runbook, API doc, DECISIONS, PROGRESS); log decisions D-142 onward.

USEFUL COMMANDS
- Offline tests: uv run pytest agents/research apps/research-console -q -m "not integration and not live"
- Lint/types: uv run ruff check . && uv run ruff format --check . ; uv run pyright agents/research ; uv run lint-imports
- Fake app for browser checks: uv run python agents/research/tests/e2e/fake_app.py --port 8140 (offline doubles; never the real app). Browser checks: uv run --with playwright python apps/research-console/tests/browser/browser_check.py
